- **adduct-annotation-via-metadata-matching**: Use when building an MSn spectral library from Orbitrap ID-X acquisitions and each file must be linked to its compound identity (adduct, in-source fragment) via a metadata table keyed on a unique sample ID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-annotation-via-metadata-matching))
- **adduct-assembly-combinatorics**: Use when building targeted lipidomics precursor/fragment transition lists from lipid class definitions and adduct rules, or when auditing how many lipid classes/molecules an in-silico lipid knowledge base covers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-assembly-combinatorics))
- **adduct-assignment-accuracy-assessment**: Use when you have a trained formula ranking model (such as MIST-CF) and want to measure the specific performance gain from incorporating multiple positive-mode adduct types (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+K]+, [M+NH4]+) instead of restricting predictions to [M+H]+ only. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-assignment-accuracy-assessment) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-assignment-mass-spectrometry**: Use when a set of tumor-correlated m/z peaks (Pearson correlation ≥ 0.7 with the tumor-class prediction map) from MALDI FT-ICR MSI data needs tentative molecular formula and adduct annotation before downstream interpretation or table export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-assignment-mass-spectrometry))
- **adduct-aware-ccs-prediction-evaluation**: Use when you need to test whether a CCS prediction model trained on one ion-mobility database (e.g. CCSBase v1.3, 16,989 values / 6,744 molecules) generalizes to a structurally different database such as METLIN-CCS (&gt;27,000 unique synthetic small-molecule structures). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-aware-ccs-prediction-evaluation))
- **adduct-aware-database-search**: Use when an imzML/mzML-derived m/z peak list from spatial metabolomics data (especially MALDI-TOF, low/medium resolution) needs metabolite annotation against HMDB or LipidMaps in a defined adduct/polarity space, and METASPACE/pySM FDR-based annotation is not applicable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-aware-database-search))
- **adduct-aware-feature-annotation**: Use when annotating LC–MS untargeted metabolomics features against a compound database in either positive or negative ionisation mode, and candidate metabolites must be scored accounting for the specific adducts they can form. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-aware-feature-annotation))
- **adduct-aware-ion-annotation**: Use when assigning metabolite identities to MS1 m/z features by querying metabolomic databases such as HMDB, and candidates must be filtered and ranked according to ionization mode, adduct knowledge, and compound properties. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-aware-ion-annotation))
- **adduct-aware-ion-species-matching**: Use when aligning multiple processed LC-HRMS target feature lists against a reference feature list where the same metabolite appears as different ion adducts, and naive m/z-only matching undercounts matched features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-aware-ion-species-matching))
- **adduct-aware-lipid-library-querying**: Use when starting lipid annotation from an MZmine/MS-DIAL/XCMS feature table (m/z, RT, intensities) and you need candidate lipid species/adducts matched by precursor exact mass before any MS/MS fragment matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-aware-lipid-library-querying))
- **adduct-based-annotation-prioritization**: Use when a list of m/z-to-compound matches (with adduct, isotope label, ppm error, and source database columns) from a MetaboShiny/MetaDBparse search must be prioritized into putative (MSI level 2) annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-based-annotation-prioritization))
- **adduct-based-feature-consolidation**: Use when after accurate mass searching has assigned multiple detected m/z features to the same metabolite via positive and negative adduct libraries, and before sample-level feature merging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-based-feature-consolidation) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-based-fragmentation-spectra-simulation**: Use when building a reference spectral library for lipid identification in LC-MS/MS and you need in silico MS/MS spectra for lipid species enumerated by class and fatty acid composition across defined adducts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-based-fragmentation-spectra-simulation))
- **adduct-based-neutral-mass-calculation**: Use when a sets workflow combines separately found positive ([M+H]+) and negative ([M-H]-) featureGroups objects with makeSet and the mixed-polarity features must be neutralized before cross-polarity grouping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-based-neutral-mass-calculation))
- **adduct-based-parent-mass-verification**: Use when cleaning annotated MS/MS library spectra whose adduct, parent mass, or precursor m/z may be inconsistent with the SMILES annotation — e.g. GNPS entries where parent mass was computed from molar mass or the adduct was misassigned. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-based-parent-mass-verification))
- **adduct-based-spectra-selection**: Use when building a benchmarking set from concatenated GNPS spectral libraries (e.g. 66,646 entries) and you need to restrict spectra to protonated [M+H]+ adducts that carry structural identifiers before InChIKey conversion and de-duplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-based-spectra-selection))
- **adduct-connection-table-generation**: Use when preparing files for a GNPS IIMN job with collapsed ion-identity edges: a filtered consensus feature map with adduct annotations must be converted into the adduct-connection supplementary table expected by GNPS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-connection-table-generation))
- **adduct-consistency-checking**: Use when enumerating within-library compound pairs from MS/MS reference libraries and a pair must be discarded unless both spectra were measured under the same protonated ion (M+H) condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-consistency-checking))
- **adduct-constrained-msms-annotation**: Use when evaluating molecular formula annotation accuracy on LC-MS/MS data with a known ground-truth adduct per compound, or when quantifying how adduct misassignment contributes to SIRIUS/ZODIAC errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-constrained-msms-annotation))
- **adduct-dimer-feature-removal**: Use when a Compound Discoverer 3.3 peak table (from .cdResult or .csv) contains redundant unidentified features that correlate with identified lipid features, and you need to filter adducts, dimers, and other spurious features before quantitation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-dimer-feature-removal))
- **adduct-filtered-spectra-split-preparation**: Use when converting MIST-predicted fingerprint probabilities plus SELFIES targets into train/val/test splits for fine-tuning a spectrum-to-structure model, and the training split must be restricted to a dominant adduct type (e.g. [M+H]+). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-filtered-spectra-split-preparation))
- **adduct-filtering-spectrum-selection**: Use when a raw spectral library contains multiple adducts, collision energies, and replicate spectra per compound, and you need one consistent binary per-compound target spectrum for binary peak-presence prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-filtering-spectrum-selection))
- **adduct-form-annotation-in-spatial-metabolomics**: Use when input is imzML/ibd MS imaging data with MS1 scans only, and the goal is structural (level 2/3) metabolite or lipid annotations — including distinguishing adduct forms of the same molecule — rather than molecular-formula-only assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-form-annotation-in-spatial-metabolomics))
- **adduct-form-evidence-annotation**: Use when annotating m/z ion peaks from an imaging MS feature matrix against a metabolite library (e.g., a 1269-metabolite mouse brain LC-MS/MS library) and you must report how many ions receive single vs. multiple annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-form-evidence-annotation))
- **adduct-form-prediction-and-mass-calculation**: Use when you have a characterized lipid species (with defined class and fatty acid composition) and need to predict which adduct forms will ionize under your experimental ionization mode (positive or negative ESI), and you require accurate precursor m/z values for DDA method configuration or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-form-prediction-and-mass-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-formation-prediction-for-metabolites**: Use when when you have unidentified LC/MS features (m/z, retention time, intensity) and need to disambiguate which metabolites they represent by accounting for the fact that observed m/z values may correspond to different adduct forms (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-formation-prediction-for-metabolites) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-fragment-feature-classification**: Use when an NP Analyst community contains multiple strong Activity-Score MS features with similar Activity and Cluster Scores but different m/z values, and you need to determine how many distinct bioactive molecules they represent. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-fragment-feature-classification))
- **adduct-fragment-formula-interpretation**: Use when after temporal correlation has identified candidate feature pairs with matching intensity profiles across time-resolved DBDI-MS experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-fragment-formula-interpretation) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-fragment-peak-pair-screening**: Use when LC/MS compound spectra contain quasi-molecular ion candidates and you want to detect analytes sharing a diagnostic adduct or neutral-loss relationship, such as choline esters identified by m/z differences of 59.074 and 221.126. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-fragment-peak-pair-screening))
- **adduct-fragment-table-construction**: Use when when initializing an mWISE annotation pipeline with a new or custom KEGG database, or when you need to reconstruct the Cpd.Add matching table with modified adduct/fragment specifications (e.g., subset to instrument-specific adducts or adjust frequency thresholds). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-fragment-table-construction) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-group-mass-difference-search**: Use when a significant-feature intensity table with columns named as AssayName_RTsec_m/z is available after MB-PLS significance testing (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-group-mass-difference-search))
- **adduct-grouping-lc-ms**: Use when processing nontargeted LC-MS data (mzML or Bruker .d) where many features are co-eluting ions of the same metabolite (isotopes, adducts, ISFs) and a deduplicated, annotated feature table is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-grouping-lc-ms))
- **adduct-informed-precursor-deconvolution**: Use when a pre-processed LC–MS/MS feature table (featureXML/mzML) with adduct information needs molecular formula and structural assignments for downstream GNPS networking or graph-view integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-informed-precursor-deconvolution))
- **adduct-intensity-aggregation-per-molecule**: Use when you have an IIMN graphml in which multiple ion species (adducts, isotopes, multimers) of the same compound appear as redundant feature nodes and you need a de-duplicated molecular-node network with aggregated intensities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-intensity-aggregation-per-molecule))
- **adduct-ion-mass-calculation**: Use when when you have derivatized metabolite structures (SMILES or mol format) and need to predict their ionization products in MS imaging, particularly when the derivatizing matrix produces non-standard adducts (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-ion-mass-calculation))
- **adduct-ion-mass-calculation-from-exact-mass**: Use when annotating untargeted LC-MS chromatographic peaks (e.g., from xcms findChromPeaks) with candidate metabolites using only MS1 m/z, by matching measured m/z to adduct-derived target m/z from exact masses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-mass-calculation-from-exact-mass))
- **adduct-ion-mass-difference-screening**: Use when a pixel-by-m/z feature matrix from an imaging MS dataset (e.g. AFAD-ESI mouse brain, 14,260 pixels × 3,044 ions) needs isotope/adduct decomposition before metabolite annotation, and the fraction of adduct vs. isotope peaks must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-mass-difference-screening))
- **adduct-ion-mass-matching**: Use when you have unlabeled LC/MS feature tables (m/z + retention time per feature) and need to estimate what fraction of features correspond to known metabolites via negative- or positive-mode adduct matching, e.g. to benchmark preprocessing methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-mass-matching))
- **adduct-ion-mz-matching**: Use when you have a preprocessed MSI feature list (measured m/z in the first column) and need to assign metabolite names and adduct forms, e.g. AFADESI-MSI liver tissue data in positive mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-mz-matching))
- **adduct-ion-pair-identification**: Use when a preprocessed spatial metabolomics peak intensity matrix (pixels x m/z ions) from MALDI/DESI-MSI is available and you need to flag which ions are adduct forms and quantify the adduct distribution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-pair-identification))
- **adduct-ion-parent-ion-pairing-analysis**: Use when when you have binned mass spectrometry imaging peaks and want to understand which detected mass-to-charge ratios represent the same metabolite in different ionization states (parent vs. adduct form). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-ion-parent-ion-pairing-analysis) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-ion-prediction-and-filtering**: Use when when annotating m/z features against a metabolite database (HMDB, Lipidmaps, etc.) and the sample preparation, ionization method, or polarity mode favors specific adduct species. For example: negative-mode LC-MS or MS imaging will preferentially generate M-H and halide adducts (M+Cl); ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-ion-prediction-and-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-ion-species-prediction**: Use when you have a list of molecular formulae for LC-IM-MS / drift tube IM-MS data and need predicted ion species (primary ion plus adducts, positive or negative mode, multiply charged) to build targeted m/z search traces. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-species-prediction))
- **adduct-ion-targeted-search-in-rt-window**: Use when the primary ion of a molecular formula has a confirmed RT window in a profile LC-DTIM-MS mzML file and you need to screen additional adduct ions (positive/negative mode), extract IM peaks, and derive DTCCS(N2) values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-targeted-search-in-rt-window))
- **adduct-ion-variant-matching**: Use when validating a molecular network's ability to recover adduct-related spectral pairs — specifically sodiated variants disconnected from protonated precursors under similarity-threshold networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ion-variant-matching))
- **adduct-ionmode-consistency-checking**: Use when parsing, standardizing, or filtering MS spectra from mixed or heterogeneous databases where adduct assignment may be manually entered, auto-inferred, or missing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-ionmode-consistency-checking) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-ionmode-cross-validation**: Use when cleaning an MS/MS spectral library where spectra carry adduct, ionmode, precursor m/z and parent mass metadata (e.g. .msp/.MGF GNPS entries) and you must detect inconsistent annotations before matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-ionmode-cross-validation))
- **adduct-isotope-confidence-rating**: Use when interpreting mummichog metabolite predictions from an untargeted LC/MS feature table: predicted compounds must be rated by the presence of expected ion forms (M+H[1+], M−H[−], adducts, C13 isotopes) before counting or reporting them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotope-confidence-rating))
- **adduct-isotope-derivative-enumeration**: Use when an untargeted LC/MS feature table (m/z, retention time, p-value) must be mapped to metabolites without MS/MS spectra, by enumerating isotopic and adduct forms (e.g. M+H[1+], M(C13)+H[1+], M+Na[1+], M−H[−]) from neutral compound formulas in a genome-scale metabolic model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotope-derivative-enumeration))
- **adduct-isotope-pattern-annotation**: Use when a processed peak list (post peak-picking/alignment, e.g. from XCMS or the DIMS SIM-stitch pipeline) needs metabolite annotations where adduct forms and isotopic abundance patterns across co-eluting/co-occurring peaks should be exploited rather than matching single masses alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotope-pattern-annotation))
- **adduct-isotope-two-tier-tree-alignment**: Use when a feature table from LC-MS untargeted metabolomics or isotope tracing must be organized into empirical compounds: pattern-matched feature pairs (e.g. 1.0034 Da 13C/12C, 22.9893 Da Na+) form a mass distance network that needs tree conversion and neutral mass inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotope-two-tier-tree-alignment))
- **adduct-isotopologue-assignment**: Use when an Asari-derived feature table must be grouped into empirical compounds (empCpds) prior to MS1 or MS2 annotation, so that isotopologues and adducts of the same metabolite are annotated as one unit. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-isotopologue-assignment))
- **adduct-level-annotation-counting**: Use when comparing coverage of two or more in-silico lipid fragmentation libraries and you need a per-lipid-class, per-adduct count matrix of library entries and annotated features to flag classes present in only one library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-level-annotation-counting))
- **adduct-mass-adjustment-calculation**: Use when you have a set of in silico-predicted compounds (with SMILES structures) and an experimental metabolomics peak list (m/z values), and you need to filter predictions to only those that could plausibly be detected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-adjustment-calculation))
- **adduct-mass-calculation**: Use when building an in-house RT/m/z metabolite library from mzML files of authentic standards and you need expected per-adduct m/z values to target EIC extraction before peak finding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-calculation))
- **adduct-mass-calculation-and-matching**: Use when when you have a list of target molecules with known molecular formulas and need to extract and validate their peaks from centroided mzML files, or when assessing whether detected peaks in a mass spectrometry run correspond to expected adducts of known metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-calculation-and-matching))
- **adduct-mass-calculation-from-smiles**: Use when when you have a metabolite SMILES structure and need to predict which adduct ions will appear in a mass spectrum acquired with a chemical derivatizing matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-calculation-from-smiles) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-calculation-positive-mode**: Use when a list of significant ion m/z values (e.g. up/down-regulated ions from an rMSIKeyIon 'ions' output list) must be assigned putative metabolite identities in positive-ion LDI/MSI data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-calculation-positive-mode))
- **adduct-mass-correction**: Use when preparing raw tandem MS/MS spectra (from NIST20/23 SDF exports or MassSpecGym TSVs) for ICEBERG/SCARF training or evaluation, where peaks must be expressed as neutral fragment masses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-correction))
- **adduct-mass-difference-matching**: Use when you have computed a histogram of pairwise mass differences from MS imaging data and need to (1) identify which observed mass differences correspond to biologically relevant or chemically known adducts, or (2) rank the most frequently observed mass differences to discover dominant adduct. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-difference-matching) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-difference-ranking**: Use when you have computed a histogram of mass differences from all pairwise mass comparisons in your MALDI-MS imaging dataset and need to prioritize which mass differences are most frequent and likely represent genuine molecular adducts (e.g., metabolite + matrix ions) rather than noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-difference-ranking))
- **adduct-mass-matching-and-clustering**: Use when after identifying statistically significant LC-MS features (e.g. via MB-VIP permutation testing) when you need to consolidate redundant measurements of the same metabolite arising from different ionisation adducts (e.g. [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M−H]−). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-matching-and-clustering) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-mass-offset-assignment**: Use when when you have an LC-MS feature table with m/z and retention time columns and need to identify which observed ions correspond to the same neutral compound under different ionization conditions and isotopic enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-offset-assignment) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-offset-parameterization**: Use when when processing LC-MS metabolomics feature tables where adduct annotation is absent or incomplete, and you need to identify which ionization adducts are present in your mass spectrometry data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-offset-parameterization) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-range-restriction**: Use when preparing a CCS training set from the McLean Unified CCS Compendium for [M-H]-, [M+H]+ or [M+Na]+ adducts and entries must be restricted to the mass ranges supported by DeepCCS, AllCCS or CCSbase. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-range-restriction))
- **adduct-mass-shift-calculation**: Use when when you have a list of observed m/z values from LC/MS feature detection and need to identify candidate metabolites by testing whether those m/z values correspond to known database compounds in specific ionization forms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-mass-shift-calculation) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-mass-shift-calculation**: Use when preparing an m/z annotation reference index from SDF-derived compound databases (ChEBI, HMDB, GNPS, LIPIDMAPS) where each neutral mass must be expanded into ion masses for the polarity and adduct set being searched. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-shift-calculation--cb65827eae8aa981))
- **adduct-mass-shift-calculation**: Use when a list of unannotated experimental m/z peaks (e.g. 200 peaks of 150-300 Da from an E. coli untargeted metabolomics dataset) must be matched against predicted compounds from a Pickaxe network expansion by enumerating adducted m/z values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-shift-calculation--a10ff33217213487))
- **adduct-mass-subtraction-from-fragment-peaks**: Use when an MS2 peak list must be annotated with subformulae of a candidate precursor formula to score formula/adduct candidates de novo — e.g. when building inputs for a formula transformer and fragmentation trees are too slow or unnecessary. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mass-subtraction-from-fragment-peaks))
- **adduct-mz-rt-conflict-detection**: Use when finalizing an in-house LC-MS metabolite standard library built with metScribeR and you must flag adduct pairs whose m/z and RT overlap within the LC-MS method's separation ability before CSV export. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mz-rt-conflict-detection))
- **adduct-mz-variant-prediction**: Use when a neutral-mass compound database (e.g. HMDB, FooDB) must be converted into an m/z-searchable annotation resource, or when matching observed peak m/z values (with a known ppm error margin) against compounds whose adduct and isotope variants are not yet enumerated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-mz-variant-prediction))
- **adduct-notation-normalization**: Use when merging spectral-library or feature-table records whose ion-identity annotations appear in inconsistent spellings (e.g. 'M + H', 'M + H +', '[M + H]+') and you need them aggregated as one ion identity before networking or counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-notation-normalization))
- **adduct-precursor-mass-calculation**: Use when a compound list supplies molecular formulas or SMILES strings (target/suspect entries) and precursor ion masses must be derived under a chosen adduct before peak extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-precursor-mass-calculation))
- **adduct-probability-feature-correlation-scoring**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics dataset carries multiple putative annotations per feature (CMM-generated annotation tables) and annotations must be prioritized for pathway analysis using adduct probability and feature-correlation evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-probability-feature-correlation-scoring))
- **adduct-probability-ranking**: Use when a RowMerger-merged annotation table contains multiple putative annotations per feature and a feature-intensity table is available, and the goal is to prioritize the most probable annotation per feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-probability-ranking))
- **adduct-redundancy-accounting**: Use when counting unique lipid ions, features, or lipid types from annotation workbooks produced by LipidMatch or comparable lipid identification software, where multiple adducts of the same lipid species appear across features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-redundancy-accounting))
- **adduct-reference-mass-matching**: Use when you have a binned mass-difference histogram from MALDI-MSI peak pairs and want to count occurrences of known adducts (Na+, K+, 13C, DHB or CHCA matrix adducts) within instrument mass tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-reference-mass-matching))
- **adduct-regex-pattern-matching**: Use when ingesting mass spectrometry spectra from heterogeneous databases or libraries where adduct annotations may be incomplete, incorrectly formatted, or inconsistent with the ionization mode. Use it before downstream analysis (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-regex-pattern-matching) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-relationship-inference**: Use when a processed, annotated LC-MS feature table (tidymass massdataset) contains suspected in-source fragmentation products misannotated as genuine metabolites and no in-pipeline deconvolution exists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-relationship-inference))
- **adduct-rule-based-ion-annotation**: Use when you have CAMERA pseudo-spectra from LC/MS data in positive or negative ion mode and need to label peaks as isotope, adduct or fragment species and derive the quasi-molecular ion mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-rule-based-ion-annotation))
- **adduct-rule-table-matching**: Use when grouping has produced CAMERA compound spectra from LC/MS peak lists (positive and/or negative ESI mode) and you need to assign ion-species annotations and propose accurate compound masses from adduct/isotope rules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-rule-table-matching))
- **adduct-series-mass-difference-validation**: Use when identify_adducts() output from a direct-injection plasma ionization (DBDI) MS dataset must be checked: candidate features should share a base analyte and differ by exact O-adduct deltas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-series-mass-difference-validation))
- **adduct-signature-identification-mass-spectrometry**: Use when you have statistically significant LC-MS features (e.g., filtered by p-value &lt; 0.01) from multi-assay metabolomics datasets and need to group features that represent the same metabolite ionized under different ESI conditions (e.g., [M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+NH4]+). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-signature-identification-mass-spectrometry) · 2 copies)
- **adduct-species-annotation**: Use when you have an untargeted LC-MS metabolomics feature table (m/z per sample) plus MS2 spectra in .mgf or .mzML format and need to assign metabolite identities to peaks at MS1 and MS2 levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-species-annotation))
- **adduct-species-simulation**: Use when you need to stress-test an LC-HRMS feature alignment tool (e.g. MetMatch) against a known ground truth by injecting artificial adduct variants and retention-time perturbations into a reference feature list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-species-simulation))
- **adduct-specific-ccs-benchmarking**: Use when you have a dataset of compounds with SMILES, an adduct type from {[M+H]+, [M-H]-, [M+Na]+}, and experimental CCS values, and need to quantitatively compare two or more CCS predictors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-specific-ccs-benchmarking))
- **adduct-specific-model-comparison**: Use when you have trained CCSP 2.0 linear SVR models for multiple adduct ion types and need to understand which Mordred descriptor classes and counts each adduct-specific model relies on. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-specific-model-comparison))
- **adduct-specific-model-fine-tuning**: Use when when you have access to annotated MS/MS spectra from a specific ionization mode (e.g., negative ESI) or adduct class (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-specific-model-fine-tuning) · 3 copies)
- **adduct-state-matching-for-ccs-comparison**: Use when experimentally measured CCS values (e.g., TWIMS from Supporting Information Tables S1/S2) must be compared against literature CCS values per metabolite/adduct pair to quantify between-laboratory deviation or justify a ±3% acceptance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-state-matching-for-ccs-comparison))
- **adduct-stratified-model-evaluation**: Use when you need to rank a machine-learning collision cross section predictor (e.g. CCSP 2.0) against alternatives like CCSbase, AllCCS or DeepCCS using held-out test splits drawn from a measured CCS compendium stratified by adduct type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/adduct-stratified-model-evaluation))
- **adduct-type-assignment-positive-mode**: Use when you have an unknown MS/MS spectrum with a measured precursor m/z and want to determine which positive-mode adduct type ([M+H]+, [M+Na]+, [M+K]+, etc.) is most likely responsible for ionization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/adduct-type-assignment-positive-mode) · 2 copies)

Triggers on ideate , refine this idea , or stress-test my plan . ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-idea-refine) · ★1)

- **addy-agent-skills-spec-driven-development**: Creates specs before coding. Use when starting a new project, feature, or significant change and no specification exists yet. Use when drafting a PRD or requirements document with objectives and scope, or when requirements are unclear, ambiguous, or only exist as a vague idea. Use when a single requirement spans several independently testable capabilities and needs decomposing into a capability map of modules before specifying. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-spec-driven-development) · ★1)
- **addy-agent-skills-test-driven-development**: Drives development with tests using the red-green-refactor loop. Use when implementing any logic, fixing any bug, or changing any behavior. Use when you need to prove that code works, when a bug report arrives, or when you're about to modify existing functionality. Use when asked to add tests to a function, fix a bug in it or change its behavior, even when the code is pasted inline and the request does not mention tests. ([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-test-driven-development) · ★1)

([Shards-foundation/shards-skills](https://github.com/Shards-foundation/shards-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/addy-agent-skills-using-agent-skills) · ★1)

- **addy-doubt-driven**: Adversarial in-flight review for high-risk decisions. Use when about to claim a non-trivial conclusion (performance gain, security fix, architectural choice, "obvious" answer) without external review.
